 2010년대까지 고유가파동/글로벌 금융 위기 등을 거치며 Air France-KLM, Delta Airlines-Northwest Airlines,
Lufthansa-Swiss International Airlines, Lufthansa-Austrian Airlines, United Airlines-Continental
Airlines, American Airlines-US Airways 등 합병 이벤트
 팬데믹 이후에도 Lufthansa의 이탈리아 국영항공사 ITA 인수(지분 41% 확보) 및 유럽 항공 그룹 IAG의 포르투갈 국영 항공사
TAP 인수(진행 중) 등 합종연횡 이벤트 꾸준

- 과거 Delta 등 서구권 FSC 합병 CASE 등으로 비춰보면 통합 대한항공의 ’27년부터의 더욱 견조한 실적 기대 有

### Page 32

P.32
[한화리서치센터]
 항공 사업의 대부분의 비용은 외부에서 조달해오는 비용
- 계약 규모가 커질수록 단위 비용 절감 가능
- 특히 기재 관련 비용(상각비, 정비비, 리스부채 이자비용 등)은 고정비성 성격이 강함
 대표적인 적용 사례는 항공기 리스
- 대한항공을 통해 5개사(대한항공/아시아나항공/진에어/에어부산/에어서울) 통합 리스 계약 체결시 계약 단가 절감
- 상각비 및 리스이자비용 등 고정성 비용 절감 효과 기대

- 미중 갈등 심화, 인천공항에서의 미주 ↔  중국 환승 수요 증가

 견조한 글로벌 항공 화물 시장
- IATA, 중동 전쟁 영향이 있었던 3월을 제외하면 올해 내내 강한 항공 화물 수요 확인 중
- 8월까지 누계 글로벌 항공 화물 수송량 YoY +4.6%, 특히 아시아 지역의 경우 YoY +7.5%
 대한항공 2Q26 항공 화물 운임 703.4원 기록 (팬데믹 화물 대란 시기 초입과 유사한 수준)
- 글로벌 기업 AI 관련 투자 확대 및 K-뷰티 수출 호조 등 유효
- 8월까지 누계 기준, 항공을 통해 수출된 반도체 제조장비(HS 코드 8486) 수출 금액 YoY +33%, 수출 중량 YoY +44%
- 8월까지 누계 기준, 항공을 통해 수출된 화장품(HS 코드 3304) 수출 금액 YoY +27%, 수출 중량 YoY +44%
 글로벌 항공기 시장 화물기 부족한 상황 지속 중. OZ 통합시 OZ 여객기 Belly Cargo를 활용한 화물 사업 효율성 증대 전망

 통합 FSC 출범 이후, 내년 3월 진에어+에어부산+에어서울의 통합 진에어 출범 예정
- 진에어:에어부산=1:0.2862684, 진에어:에어서울=1:0.7501939 신주 교부
- ’25년 기준 국제선 수송객 단순 합산시, 3사 점유율은 13.2%로 아시아나항공 실적 소폭 상회
- 3사 보유 기재 합산시 총 60대 (참고: 아시아나항공 보유 기재 66대)
- 진에어는 보유 중대형기 B777-200 반납 예정이며, 10월부터 A321-200 기종 노선 투입. 해당 A321-200(A321ceo) 기재는
아시아나항공으로부터 인도받은 기종 (에어부산 및 에어서울 기재는 전부 에어버스 생산 기종)
- 3분기 중 대한항공 및 아시아나항공으로부터 소형기 임차. A321neo 10대(대한항공), B737-900 3대(대한항공), A321ceo 1대
(대한항공). 지난해, 조원태 회장은 A321neo 기종을 통합 LCC 주력 기종으로 활용하겠다 밝힘

 트리니티항공의 지속되는 적자
- 2Q24부터 4Q25까지 7개 분기 연속 영업적자 기록. ‘24년까지 상장 LCC 전반적으로 견조한 실적 기록한 것과 상반되는 흐름
- LCC의 기본 모델은 기종 단일화 및 노선 단일화를 통한 규모의 경제 <-> 트리니티항공의 장거리 진출 전략과는 다소 거리 有
- 장거리 국제선 여객 시장 진출에 따른 고정비 부담 증가로 해석
- 한편 장거리 국제선 여객 시장의 경우 상용/환승/프리미엄 수요 중심의 시장으로 변화되며 트리니티항공의 저가 전략과 miss match
 지속된 영업적자는 재무 리스크로 연결
- ‘25년 8월, 유상증자(1,100억원) + 무상감자 + 영구전환사채발행(400억원) + 신주인수권부사채발행(500억원) 등으로 자금 수혈
- ‘26년 3월, 약 700억원 규모의 유상증자 한차례 더 단행
- 소노그룹의 꾸준한 자금 수혈 이뤄지고 있으나 매크로 변동성 확대되는 시기, 이전부터 지속되어 온 영업 적자 상황은 다소 부정적

자료: 전자공시시스템, 한화투자증권 리서치센터자료: 전자공시시스템, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

 이스타 항공 소유주 VIG파트너스의 에어프레미아 인수 추진
- 에어프레미아, 9월 1100억원 규모 유증으로 완전자본잠식 일부 해소되나 향후 자본 확충 이벤트 1-2차례 더 필요할 것. 이는 실 소유
주인 김정규 회장 경영 공백 상황을 감안할 때 부담으로 작용할 전망
- VIG파트너스는 통합 진에어 출범 이후 LCC 시장 재편을 대비, 몸집을 키워 엑싯하는 전략을 가져가는 것으로 추정
- 양측의 매각 사유와 매입 니즈가 확고하기 때문에 인수 가격만 맞다면 가까운 시일 내 에어프레미아 손바뀜 전망
- 중단거리 노선의 이스타항공 + 미주 장거리 노선의 에어프레미아 결합시 환승 시너지 및 비용 절감 등을 기대한 인수 추진으로 추정
- 다만 기존 중단거리에서 장거리로 확장한 트리니티항공의 경우 아직까지 환승 시너지는 확인되지 않고 있으며 비용은 오히려 확대되며 지
속적인 자본 확충 要. 시장에서도 트리니티항공의 부진을 지켜봤기 때문에 이스타+에어프레미아 통매각까지 시간 소요 전망

P.55
[한화리서치센터]
 일본 노선은 전체 국제선 노선 가운데 가장 수요가 좋은 노선  1Q26 LCC 호실적의 원동력
- 강한 수요로 국내 지방發 주요 대도시는 물론, 지방 소도시 신규 취항 등 공급 확대
- 에어로케이는 청주發 일본 12개 노선, 주당 99회 운영. 주요 대도시 외 이바라키/마쓰야마/오비히로 등 지방 소도시까지 취항
- 이스타항공은 김해發 일본 5개 노선, 주당 51회 운영. 에어프레미아는 오는 12월부터 인천-삿포로 노선 신규 취항
 제주항공의 궁극적인 일본 노선 독식 예상
- 내년에도 일본 노선 수요는 강할 전망. 공급 경쟁 여전할 수 있으나 본업에만 집중할 수 있는 제주항공의 M/S 확대 전망
- 통합 진에어의 경우 내년 한 해 내/외부 통합 등 본업 외 신경 써야할 부분 多
- 비상장 LCC의 경우 이스타+에어프레미아 통합, 파라타항공 재무 건전성 확보 및 미주 노선 진출, 에어로케이 재무 건전성 확보 등

일본 노선 경쟁에만 집중하긴 어려울 전망
그리고 끝나지 않은 LCC 구조조정 당분간 승자는 제주항공
자료: 에어포탈, 한화투자증권 리서치센터자료: 에어포탈, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

- ’25년 전반적인 유가 수준 하락 및 연료효율성 증대된 신기재 도입 등으로 유류비 비중 축소: ’24년 31.6% > ’25년 28.9%

- 2Q26 분기 평균 환율은 1달러 = 1,501.9원

(1mth lagging 기준 2Q26 $182.9  3Q26 $158.5)
- 1달러 환율 분기 평균 2Q26 1,501.9원  3Q26 1,474.8원
 유가 및 환율 부담 완화에 따라 3Q26 주요 항공사 유류비 부담도 QoQ 크게 완화 추정
- 대한항공 1조 7,197억원, 영업비용 37%
- 진에어 1,656억원, 영업비용 36%

- 에어서울및 에어부산통합에 따른 비용 영업단에반영되며(과거 대한항공사례와유사 전망) 단기 실적 부담 있을 것

Compliance Notice
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할
재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 기관투자자 또는 제3자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박수영)
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부
터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사용될 수 없습니다.

당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예
상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종목별로 다릅니다.
산업 투자의견
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한

흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전망은 해당 산업 내 분석
대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.
당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 (기준일: 2026년 9월 30일)
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계
금융투자 상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

### Page 1

2026년 10월 08일 I 산업분석_Update 
통신 장비 
트럼프 1기/2기 중국산 장비 규제 차이점 분석

트럼프 1기 당시엔 화웨이 장비 저지에 규제 정책이 집중 
지난 2019년 5G 도입 당시 미국에선 중국 통신 장비 규제가 본격화되면서 시장에 큰 파문이 
일어났다. 규제의 핵심은 화웨이 장비의 미국 진입을 불허한다는 것이었다. 제재 명분은 군사 
보안 이슈었지만 미/중간 4차 산업 패권 경쟁이 결정적이었다. 통신장비 업종에서의 높은 화웨
이 위상이 경계감을 작용시켰다고 볼 수 있겠다. 하지만 트럼프 1기엔 통신 장비에 대한 규제
는 있었지만 통신 부품까지 규제하지는 않았다. 이런 이유로 중국 부품들이 미국의 대중국 제
재에도 불구하고 글로벌 SI 공급업체로 선정되는 경우가 다반사였었는데 대표적으로는 대부과
기, 우한핑구 등을 들 수 있겠다. 미국은 2020년/2021년 주파수 경매 이후 2021~2022년 통신
장비 호황기를 맞이하였다. 하지만 이 시기 중국 화웨이와 대부과기/우한핑구는 전혀 다른 매
출액 흐름을 나타내는데 화웨이는 중국 규제 영향을 받은 반면 대부과기/우한핑구와 같은 부품 
회사들은 직접적인 규제를 피했기 때문이었다. 규제를 피한 중국 부품 회사들은 글로벌 SI로의 
공급을 통해 미국 시장에서 수혜를 받게 되는데 특히 에릭슨의 상대적 수혜가 컸다. 진입이 어
려울 것으로 예상되었던 삼성전자에도 벤더 내 M/S 상으론 높은 점유율을 기록하였다. 
트럼프 2기엔 부품까지 규제 확대되는 양상, 광에서 무선까지 확산될 전망 
그런데 트럼프 2기 출범 이후엔 변화된 분위기가 감지된다. 통신장비뿐만 아니라 부품에까지 
규제가 행해지고 있기 때문이다. 공급 부족 이슈로 인해 비록 연기되기는 했지만 2026년 9월 
미국 FCC가 중국 이노라이트, 이옵토링크를 제재 대상에 올린 바 있고 미국 의회에서도 관련 
규제가 논의 중이다. 이런 분위기로 볼 때 2027년 무선 통신 장비에도 중국산 부품 규제가 행
해질 가능성은 충분하다. 광 모듈처럼 미국 업체들이 중국과 직접적인 경쟁 관계에 있진 않지
만 중국업체들의 통신장비 업종에서의 위상 강화가 부담스럽기 때문이다. 결국은 미국/중국간 
피지컬 AI 경쟁이 본격화될 것이란 점을 감안할 때 그렇다. 
삼성전자와 더불어 에릭슨 선택에 주목, KMW/RFHIC 큰 수혜 전망 
만약 광통신에 이어 무선 부문에서도 미국의 통신 장비 부품 규제가 이루어진다면 국내 아웃소
싱 장비/부품 업체들의 경우엔 큰 수혜가 예상된다. 지난 20년간 글로벌 SI간 합병 및 M/S 변
화가 나타나기도 했지만 아웃소싱 부품/장비사의 경우엔 더욱 급격한 업종 전환 및 M&A가 이
루어졌기 때문이다. 10년전 대비 아웃소싱 통신 장비 업체들의 숫자는 절반 이하로 줄어든 반
면 친미 국가들의 중국 장비 제재는 심화되는 양상이다. 현 시점에서는 미국 통신사 벤더로 선
정될 가능성이 높은 에릭슨과 더불어 삼성전자의 아웃소싱 장비/부품업체 선정에 관심을 높일 
필요가 있겠다. 트럼프 1기 당시와는 달리 중국산 부품을 다수 사용하는데 따른 부담이 커질 
수 있기 때문이다. 삼성전자 내 RFHIC와 KMW 납품 비중은 확대될 가능성이 높고 에릭슨의 
RFHIC와 KMW 신규 벤더 선정 가능성이 높아졌다는 판단이다. 현재 주가 수준을 감안하면 
KMW/RFHIC에 대한 적극 매수 대응을 추천한다.

Overweight

Neutral(중립)_목표주가가 현주가 대비 -15%~15% 등락 
Reduce(비중축소)_목표주가가 현주가 대비 -15% 이상 하락 가능 
 산업의 분류 
Overweight(비중확대)_업종지수가 현재지수 대비 15% 이상 상승 여력 
Neutral(중립)_업종지수가 현재지수 대비 -15%~15% 등락 
Underweight(비중축소)_업종지수가 현재지수 대비 -15% 이상 하락 가능

### Page 1

2026-10-02 
Economy Brief

수출과 코스피지수간 괴리 현상은 마무리 국면 Economy Brief 
[경제] 박상현 2122-9196 shpark@imfnsec.com

Check Point 
국내 수출이 폭발적인 증가세를 유지 중이며 4분기에도 이러한 수출 기조는 이어질 것으로 예상       
한국과 더불어 글로벌 반도체 수출을 견인하는 대만과 비교하면 국내 코스피지수는 수출 흐름대비 상대적으로 
부진함. 대외 불확실성 요인도 있지만 한국과 대만의 환율 흐름도 일부 영향을 미친 것으로 판단    
환율효과 등 여러 대내외 요인으로 강력한 수출 호조와는 다소 엇박자를 보였던 코스피지수 흐름이 4분기에는 
다시 수출 흐름을 따라가는 모습으로 복귀할 것으로 예상함

이정표를 매월 기록 중인 국내 수출 슈퍼 사이클   
국내 수출이 폭발적인 증가세를 유지하고 있다. 시장 예상을 큰 폭으로 상회하는 강력한 수출 모멘텀이 이어지고 있다. 9월 국내 
수출은 추석 연휴에 따른 조업일수 감소에도 불구하고 전년동월 83.5% 급증한 1,200억 달러를 기록했다. 지난 6월 월간 수출액이 
1,000억달러를 넘어선 이후 3개월만에 월간 수출액이 1,200억 달러를 상회한 것이다. 1~9월 누적 수출액은 8,145억 달러로 
지난해 연간 수출액 7,093억 달러를 넘어섰다. 특히, 국내 코스피지수와 높은 상관관계를 보이는 일 평균 수출액은 조업일수 감소 
영향이 일부 있지만 사상 처음으로 50억 달러(9월 일 평균 수출액 56.3억 달러)를 넘어서는 등 국내 수출 규모가 또 다시 한 단계 
도약했음을 뒷받침해주었다. 
이처럼 국내 수출이 초슈퍼 사이클 행진을 이어가고 있는 중심에는 반도체 수출이 있다. 9월 반도체 수출액은 전년동월 263% 
폭증한 603억 달러를 기록했다. 지난해 국내 월 평균 수출규모가 591억 달러였음을 고려할 때 반도체 수출 모멘텀이 얼마나 
강력한지를 가늠해 볼 수 있다. 반도체 수출 급증으로 9월 전체 수출액에서 반도체가 차지하는 비중은 49.9%에 이르게 되었다. 
중요한 것은 반도체를 중심으로 한 국내 수출 호조가 당분간 이어질 공산이 높다는 점이다. 당사의 지난 9월 29일 보고서(4Q 국내 
수출도 순항)에서도 지적한 바와 같이 국내 수출경기와 높은 연관성을 보이는 4분기 수출산업경기전망지수(EBSI)는 111.3으로 
3분기 107.0대비 4.3pt 상승했다. 4분기 수출 역시 양호한 흐름을 이어질 것임을 예고하고 있다.  
무엇보다 반도체 수출이 강한 모멘텀을 유지할 공산이 크다. <그림2>에서 보듯 이미 이전 반도체 슈퍼사이클과 비교하기 어려울 
정도로 이번 반도체 수출사이클이 강력한 추세를 보이고 있는 상황에서 미국 경기 호조를 지탱하는 AI 투자사이클이 더욱 강해지고 
있기 때문이다. 또한, <그림3>에서 확인되듯이 국내 반도체 업황 사이클, 즉 반도체 출하-재고 사이클이 2분기~3분기초 다소 
둔화되는 양상을 보였지만 8월을 기점으로 둔화국면에서 벗어나고 있다. 강력한 반도체 빅사이클 흐름 속에서 반도체 업황이 일시적 
소순환 조정국면을 거치고 다시 강한 확장사이클에 재진입하고 있는 것이다. 
반도체뿐만 아니라 반도체제외 업종의 수출액 증가도 고무적이며 4분기 수출 호조에 기여할 것이다. <그림6>의 반도체 제외 수출액 
추이를 보더라도 9월 반도체제외 업종의 수출규모가 큰 폭으로 증가하고 있다. 참고로 9월 반도체제외 업종의 수출증가율은 
전년동월 23.1%를 기록했다.

수출 증가율 흐름과 비교되는 최근 국내 주가지수 흐름   
반도체 수출을 중심으로 국내 수출이 예상보다 더욱 강력한 증가율을 기록 중이지만 국내 코스피지수 흐름은 다소 답답한 횡보세를 
이어가고 있다. 특히, 한국과 더불어 글로벌 반도체 수출을 견인하는 대만과 비교하더라도 국내 코스피지수는 수출 흐름대비

둔화/하강
회복/상승
26년 8월
상대적으로 부진한다. <그림8>의 한국과 대만의 수출액 대비 양국의 시가총액 비율을 비교해 보더라도 대만에 비해 한국 ‘코스피 
시가총액/수출액’ 비율이 상대적으로 부진하다. 구조적으로 반도체를 중심으로 한 IT 수출비중이 큰 대만과 달리 한국 수출의 경우 
업종별로 다변화되어 있음이 이러한 차이를 유발시킬 수도 있지만 또 다른 요인으로 환율 영향도 일부 작용한 것으로 판단된다. 
3분기 중 달러-원 환율은 12% 하락, 즉 원화가 급격한 강세를 보인 반면에 달러-대만 달러 환율은 동기간 보합세를 유지했다.  
3분기 원화가 급격한 강세를 보이는 동안 대만달러를 별다른 변화를 보이지 않은 것이다. 물론 환율이 양국 주가지수 흐름에 결정적 
차이를 유발하는 요소는 아니지만 지난해 하반기 이후 ‘달러-원 환율과 코스피지수/대만 가권지수 상대강도’는 <그림9>에서 보듯 
달러-원 환율과 높은 상관관계를 보여주고 있다. 양국의 환율 흐름 차별화가 일정부분 양국 주가 흐름에도 영향을 주고 있는 것으로 
여겨진다. 
중요한 것은 향후 흐름인데 4분기중 원화가 추가 강세 강세를 보인다고 해도 강세폭은 3분기에 비해 대폭 축소될 것으로 예상된다. 
따라서 4분기 중 환율, 즉 원화 강세 현상이 국내 증시에 미치는 영향은 3분기대비 크게 약화될 것이다. 또한, 24년 이후 
코스피지수와 대만 가권지수의 상승 폭을 보면 최근 동일한 수준에 이르렀다. 이는 대만 가권지수와 코스피지수가 차별화보다는 
동조화 추세가 강해질 수 있음을 의미한다. 
환율효과 등 여러 대내외 요인으로 강력한 수출 호조와는 다소 엇박자를 보였던 코스피지수 흐름이 4분기에는 다시 수출 흐름을 
따라가는 모습으로 복귀할 것으로 예상한다.

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

India: Degrees of rupee defence

16 October 2026 
Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

美元/桶 %
► 美國經濟仍具韌性 ，惟通膨壓力猶存 。觀察
美國9月ISM服務業指數表現 ，內需活動維持
穩健，企業投入成本壓力亦有所升溫；另一
方面，受地緣政治因素影響 ，美國柴油價格
大幅攀升 ，而柴油為卡車運輸 、農業及製造
業的重要燃料 ，若價格持續維持高檔 ，恐進
一步增添通膨壓力 。考量聯準會偏鷹立場 ，
且當前通膨降溫進程不如預期 ，年底前仍保
有升息空間。
► 美國債務總額突破40兆美元，預估2026年公
眾持有聯邦債務占 GDP的比率將達 101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫 。與此同時 ，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮 ，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性 ，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險 ，要求更高的風險溢酬 。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點 。隨長天期債券利率續揚 ，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭 ，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示 ，眾議院選情
較為明朗，民主黨有超過9成的機率控制眾議
院。但參議院選情則尚有變數 ，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先 ，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚 ，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期 ，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言 ，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異 ，潛在受惠產業亦有不同 ，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。
► 另一方面 ，油價高檔徘迴 ，導致年底前通 膨
擔憂仍存 ，加上AI企業發債需求成長 、美國
財政體質惡化等 因素共同發酵 ，推動近期 美
債殖利率逐步攀高 ，並令股市預期本益 比壓
力延續 ，進而限縮了投資人短期對美股的追
價動能，短期指數恐維持震盪輪動格局 。可
留意抗波動 、體質佳且具價值優勢之標的 ，
待期中選舉結束與3Q26財報展開後，不確定
性減退將有助資金回歸長線AI題材。
殖利率攀升令美股本益比面臨壓力參議院期中選舉選情仍具變數
期中選前擾動與殖利率走高，美股短期恐仍震盪輪動

### Page 7

7
投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息 1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率 ，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標 。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性 ，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱 態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬 提升已成為 支撐股價的
重要結構性因素 。另一方面 ，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度 ，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊 。因此 ，隨BoJ延
續升息方向 ，將擴大銀行淨利差 ，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益 。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯 ，然日本
銀行股 在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升 ，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價

BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠

### Page 8

8
股市
 公債殖利率走高雖可能壓抑 股債市場近期 動能，然而觀察美股第三季獲利年增 率有望仍為雙位數成
長，或支撐股市表現 。市場持續關注AI資本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場
動盪，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評
價面壓力等紛擾 帶來干擾，可布局防禦 股增強抗震力 。長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且
基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融及醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨 發展。殖利率偏高環境建議以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走
升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸

引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期抗波動，逢回可分批布局仍具長線趨勢之AI與科技產業

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

Document 752: Lagarde’s comments point to a December ECB rate hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Lagarde’s comments point to a December ECB rate hike.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Upside risks to inﬂation and downside risks to growth clearly
complicate the ECB's job. However, comments at today's press
conference suggest the ECB is set to hike again in December
Carsten Brzeski
ECB President Christine Lagarde addresses a press conference following
today's meeting
Rates on hold and Lagarde out. That’s the headline from today’s
ECB meeting. But look beyond that, and the message is more
nuanced: despite growing stagﬂationary pressures, the ECB still

looks set to hike rates again in December.
The decision to keep interest rates unchanged was not a surprise.
The main argument behind this decision is that there are currently
few knock-on eﬀects from higher headline inﬂation, while ﬁnancing
conditions, mainly bond yields, have become more restrictive.
A pause after two rate hikes this year was expected, even if macro
developments since the last meeting could have justiﬁed another
rate hike. Headline inﬂation increased again in September, and

### Page 2

most leading indicators point to continued resilience in the
eurozone economy. However, the surge in bond yields on the back
of the French crisis is already imposing a new restrictive monetary
policy stance. Or in other words, the bond market has taken over
the ECB’s job.
According to Christine Lagarde, risks to the eurozone’s growth
outlook remain tilted to the downside, while risks to the inﬂation
outlook are tilted to the upside. A combination of risks that clearly

complicates the ECB’s path ahead. This dilemma was underscored
by her observation that key developments since the September
meeting have been a further rise in energy prices and a worsening
of ﬁnancing conditions.
Despite these new complications, the ECB apparently debated a
rate hike today. However, the worsening of ﬁnancing conditions
took the option oﬀ the table.
Lagarde also emphasised the importance of indirect and second-
round eﬀects from higher energy prices. Currently, the ECB is not

seeing any additional second-round eﬀects, other than the ones
included in the September staﬀ projections.
ECB looks set to hike again in December
despite increasing stagﬂationary pressures
Looking ahead, there are three main parameters that, in our view,
will determine the ECB’s path going forward:
1. The length and intensity of the war in the Middle East. The
longer oil prices remain elevated, the higher the probability of
more rate hikes.
2. The knock-on eﬀects of higher energy prices. So far, there has

hardly been any pass-through of higher energy prices in the
rest of the economy. If this remains the case, the need for
further rate hikes drops. If it doesn’t, the possibility of rate hikes
rises.
3. France. Any solution to France's current ﬁscal woes could bring
some relief to bond markets and allow the ECB to continue
hiking rates. Without any solution, bond markets have taken

over the ECB’s role, making rate hikes less likely (and less

With today’s comments, another rate hike at the ECB’s December

meeting is clearly in the oﬃng. It would require a sharp worsening
of the growth outlook to probably prevent the ECB from hiking once
again.
In short, with downside risks to the growth outlook and upside risks
to the inﬂation outlook, the ECB seems to be increasingly
concerned about stagﬂationary forces, without calling them
stagﬂationary. Remember back in April, Lagarde called ‘stagﬂation’
a ‘ﬂashy term’.

Document 777: Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01

Economy 
Regional Roundup: Coast-to-coast growth 
29 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Bank of America aggregated credit and debit card spending per household is growing at healthy rates across the country. 
Discretionary spending growth has improved markedly relative to the last two years. While the West has led recently and the 
South has lagged somewhat, no region appears to be experiencing a meaningful consumer slowdown. 
• The South has seen softer discretionary retail spending, particularly in home-related durable goods categories. However, the 
region continues to post the strongest after-tax wage growth and has been the largest contributor to growth in new business 
applications nationwide. 
• Business formation is occurring across a wide range of industries, with professional services, retail, healthcare, construction and 
hospitality all accounting for meaningful shares of new business applications. While entrepreneurial activity is broad-based 
across industries nationwide, industry revenue mixes vary considerably by region.

Spending growth is solid across all regions  
Spending growth was strongest in the West in September, while spending growth lagged in the Northeast and South, though not 
by much. Total credit and debit card spending per household increased around 5.6% year-over-year (YoY) in the West, outpacing 
other regions by less than a percentage point, according to Bank of America aggregated card data (Exhibit 1). All regions also 
experienced major improvements in spending growth compared to last year, increasing by over three percentage points in every 
region.   
Spending growth in the Northeast peaked at around 6% YoY in June but remained a solid 4.8% YoY in September. The slowest 
growth was in the South, though spending was still up nearly 4.8% YoY.  
Exhibit 1: Spending growth has mostly improved since the start of the year across all regions 
Total card spending per household by US Census region (three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Discretionary spending growth was also somewhat weaker in the Northeast and South than in the Midwest and West (Exhibit 2). 
However, these two regions have also seen the most improvement in “nice-to-have” spending over the past two years. And here 
spending was also up around 4.8% YoY in both regions, still a very healthy rate of growth.

Durables spending is weighing on discretionary retail spending in the South 
One of the key themes from September in the overall spending data was a broadening in spending growth, particularly in “more 
discretionary” retail categories like clothing, electronics, building materials and non-store/miscellaneous retailers (read more in 
Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers). Bank of America card data shows that this improvement is occurring across all 
regions.  
However, the South lags, though this is mostly due to declines in spending on home-related durables in, for instance, home 
improvement stores and at furniture retailers. In our view, this could reflect some slowdown in domestic migration to the Sun 
Belt and a corresponding easing in home/moving-related spending (read more in On the move: Renovation over relocation?) 
Exhibit 3: The Midwest and Northeast have seen a higher share of discretionary retail categories with spending growth when compared to the 
South and West 
Diffusion index across “more discretionary” retail categories by spending across US Census regions (monthly, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The South leads in after-tax wage and business application growth 
One reason we are not overly concerned about consumer spending in the South is the strength of after-tax wage growth in the 
region. Bank of America deposit data suggests that after-tax wage growth was strongest there, though only by a slim margin, up 
nearly 4.2% YoY, just ahead of households in the Midwest, and outpacing the West and Northeast by less than half a percentage 
point (Exhibit 4).

Beyond wage income, business ownership offers another potential channel for wealth accumulation. The South has been the 
primary contributor to overall US growth in new business applications so far in 2026, according to US Census Bureau data 
(Exhibit 5).  
While historically only a small share of these applications is likely to become a business employing workers, this surge in 
applications points to a potential pipeline of entrepreneurial activity. For businesses that become established and profitable, 
ownership can provide a pathway to wealth through retained earnings and the accumulation of business equity. Still, the extent 
of that opportunity depends on businesses’ survival, profitability and financing needs (read more in Business Checkpoint: Looking 
into logistics).

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Business application growth is widespread across regions and industries 
Looking at 2026 business applications by industry, entrepreneurial activity spans multiple industry groupings (Exhibit 6). 
Professional, scientific, technical and other services account for the largest share, followed by retail trade and the grouping 
comprising healthcare, education services and the arts. Additionally, businesses provide a significant source of income for US 
households. In fact, business revenues accounted for nearly 14% of total pre-tax income, according to the US Census’ 2025 
Survey of Consumer Finances.

Combined, this suggests that potential pathways to business ownership extend not only across various households (read more on 
generational shifts in entrepreneurship in July’s A new generation of business formation) but also across many industries, rather 
than a single sector. 
While business applications show the breadth of prospective entrepreneurial activity nationwide, the industry composition of 
existing business revenue offers a regional lens on business activity. Exhibit 7 shows how revenue was distributed across 
industries within regions according to the 2024 Annual Integrated Economic Survey from the US Census Bureau, highlighting the 
extent to which regional economies are concentrated in particular sectors. 
Although wholesale trade was the sector with the largest share of revenue in each region in the US, there is notable variance in 
economic activity from business revenues across the country. For example, finance and insurance accounted for more than 20% 
of revenue in the Northeast, whereas it’s about half that in the West. Also, manufacturing was only 8.5% of revenue in the 
Northeast, and more than 20% in the Midwest. Overall, the data shows a dynamic national business landscape, with broad 
entrepreneurial activity but distinct regional differences in the industries generating revenue.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend includes total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs. Value grocers is defined using a combination of MCC code and proprietary methods.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1 -3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressu res, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect dir ect references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell -off and a Eurozone -
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisat ion of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026 –FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient expo rt 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy , slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following  
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full -year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12 –13% yoy , close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double -digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy  in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI -related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak s pot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.

Document 801: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conf lict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising  force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the  rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid -September for middle distillates 2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre , Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near -maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region 3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estim ates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base -case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production leve ls, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre -winter storage). I n a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillov er effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

欣興 3037 TT  ( 電子–電子零組件 ) 
欣然高飛，蓄勢騰飛 
投資與評價 
1. 欣興 3Q26 受惠 ABF 載板、HDI 業務等高階 AI 產品比重提升，本業創新
高；4Q26 預期載板價格提升幅度仍將持續擴大，營運仍將續創新高。 
2. 預期載板漲價由成本轉為需求驅動，高階ABF 載板產能在 AI 晶片需求提
升下出現供不應求，欣興仍將是眾多 AI 客戶的供應商首選，營運將持續
走高，預估 26 年 EPS 24.93 元、27 年 EPS 43.15 元。 
3. 目前欣興股價為 1,060 元，約為 24.6 x FY27E EPS ，位於近 3 年 PER 參
考區間 10~40x 平均值位置。考量欣興持續加大 26 年資本投入且集中於
高階 ABF 載板擴充 ，公司營運重心持續向高階載板產品推疊，包含
CoWoS、EMIB-T 等先進封裝平台，整體獲利結構大幅提升，故維持買進
投資評等 ，因調整 27 年獲利預估，目標價上修至 1,300 元(30 x 27(F)PER)。 
重點更新 
3Q26 ABF 載板漲幅持續擴大，欣興本業創歷史新高 
欣興 3Q26 受益於 ABF 載板價格漲幅持續擴大，加上光復一廠三期邁入量產
貢獻；HDI 在新設備進駐調整之下，出貨於美系客戶下世代產品貢獻營收。整
體欣興 3Q26 營收為 526.50 億元(+22.8% QoQ)，受惠 AI 高階佔比持續增
加，毛利率達 28.8%(+4.0 ppts QoQ)，與原估相符。儘管因研發費用投入多
於預期，營業費用率為 8.9%(-0.5 ppts QoQ)，略微不及原預估，惟營業淨利
仍因營運規模擴大季增 58.3%達 105.28 億元，加上因金融評價資產帶動業外
收入達 26.36 億元，稅後淨利為 100.52 億元(-23.4% QoQ，+358.2% YoY)，
EPS 6.36 元，本業仍續創新高。

ABF 價格與產能持續提升帶動下，欣興 4Q26 營運將再創新高 
考量 4Q26 ABF 載板價格因受原物料以及產能瓶頸而將維持漲價幅度，加上
新廠產能擴充以及既有產能去瓶頸 ， 帶動整體營運維持高增長， 維持欣興4Q26
營收與原估相近之 592.23 億元(+12.5% QoQ)，並在持續提升產能利用率與
產品組合下，上修毛利率預期至 32.4%(+3.5 ppts QoQ )。預期營業淨利為
143,72 億元(+36.5% QoQ)，帶動稅後淨利將季增 11.9%達 112.51 億元，
EPS 6.84 元，營運將續創新高。

姜仁匡 / PCB 
+886-2-2326-9899#1601 / kenchiang@cathayfut.com.tw

Document 899: バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

Source PDF: all report pdf/バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 6 日

MURC Focus

バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

～10 月末発表の秋季予算案で具体的な財源を示せるか～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 伝統的な財政拡張を訴えるバーナム新政権 
英国では、9 月 27 日から 30 日にかけて、北西部の主要都市リバプールで与党である労働党の党大会が開催
された。7 月 20 日に就任したアンディ・バーナム首相にとって初の党大会となると同時に、新政権の財政運営方
針が示されるという点で、この党大会は非常に高い注目を浴びた。この場で示されたのは、中道左派である労働
党らしい、伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。

具体的には、バーナム 新政権の関係閣僚らは、政府調達（政府が民間からモノやサービスを購入すること）の
増強による景気浮揚策を主張した。例えばジョン・ヒーリー財務相は 28 日の党大会で、スコットランドのクライド海
軍基地に浮きドック 3 基を建設する他、海洋調査船を建造することに数十億ポンドを投資する計画を発表した。
いずれも国内の業者に発注することで雇用や所得につなげる方針である。

また労働党大会に先立つ 26 日、バーナム新政権は、新築住宅を初めて購入する国民向けに新たな住宅購
入支援策を導入すると発表した。保守党政権下の 2013 年から 2023 年にかけて実施された“Help to Buy”と似
たスキームであり、購入者は購入価格の 2.5%に相当する頭金を支払えば、政府が購入価格の 20%までを融資
するというものである。

このように、バーナム新政権が目指す方針は、労働党に伝統的な“大きな政府”による財政拡張路線を反映し
ている。そうした点で、バーナム新政権の方針はキア・スターマー前政権とはあまり変化がない。一方で、新政権
が示した財政拡張路線が金融市場の評価を得るためには、財源を明示する必要がある。10 月 28 日に発表する
秋季予算案で、金融市場が納得する内容を示せるかが鍵を握る。

〇9月末にリバプールで開催された労働党の党大会で、バーナム新政権の財政運営方針が示されたが 、それ
は中道左派である労働党らしい伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。 
〇一方、金融市場 では、それまではバーナム 人気によって労働党 の政権運営が安定するとの思惑から“英国
買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。 
〇増税は有権者の反発を、国債発行が投資家の反発を生むと予想される中、秋季予算で財政拡張の財源の
筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。

### Page 2

2. 財政拡張を続けてきた近年の英国 
バーナム新政権は財政拡張に活路を 見いだそうとしているが、これまでも英国の政権は財政拡張に臨んでき
た（図表 1）。例えば英国の実質 GDP（国内総生産）に占める公需（＝政府支出＋公的固定資本形成）は、保守
党政権（テリーザ・メイ元首相）期の 2018 年後半に 21.5%を下回るまで低下したが、以降は上昇し、直近 2026
年 4～6 月時点で 24.4%となっている（図表 1）。

この間、2020 年 1～3 月期に生じたコロナショック後の公需の減退も限定的にとどまっており、結局は公需が
GDP に占める割合は“V 字回復”することとなった。労働党が政権を奪取したのは 2024 年 7 月の総選挙だが、
それ以前の保守党政権下でも、英国は事実上、財政拡張を続けていたことになる。その結果、公的債務残高の
対 GDP 比率も、一時は高インフレのために低下したが、再び上昇した（図表 2）。

このように近年の英国は、保革の立場を問わず財政拡張を続けてきた。しかし、EU 離脱やエネルギー不安に
伴う供給制約の高まりの中でそうした手法を取り続けたため 、英国はスタグフレーション（景気停滞と物価高進の
併存）に突入した。その改善のために必要な処方箋は、規制緩和を通じた供給の刺激である。しかし、それは“小
さな政府”を追求することにもなるため、中道左派として“大きな政府”を目指す労働党は容認できない。

バーナム新首相は地方の主導の経済成長を訴え、そうした意味での分権化に意欲的と考えられる。 ただし労
働党としては、公需による民需の刺激を通じた景気浮揚を引き続き志向していることが、秋の党大会で示された
形である。一方、金融市場では、バーナム新首相就任直後は、首相の人気に支えられて新政権の運営が安定す
るとの思惑から“英国買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。

### Page 3

3. 財源なき財政拡張で信用を失うリスク 
スターマー前政権もまた、財政拡張路線による景気浮揚を目指した が、同時にそれは、供給の底上げを狙うも
のでもあった。老朽インフラ（鉄道や水道など）の再開発はその最たるものである。またスターマー前政権は、財政
規律にも配慮し、財源を国債発行よりも増税でカバーし、 財政拡張策の整合性を取ろう とした。財源を示したこと
を投資家は評価したが、増税に有権者が反発したため、結局、スターマー前政権は短命に終わった。

スターマー前政権下で低迷した労働党の支持率は、バーナム人気に支えられ“V 字回復”し、右派の改革党
（UK Reform ）と肉薄するに至る（図表 3）。労働党としては打ち出した目玉政策を着実に遂行し、有権者の支持
をさらに確たるものにしたいところである。とはいえ、そうした目玉政策は財政拡張を伴うものであるため、財源をど
う確保するか明確な筋道を示さなければ、金融市場の動揺を誘う。

すでに英国の長期金利（10 年国債流通利回り）は、グローバルなインフレ加速懸念に伴う世界的な金利上昇
の波に飲まれ、上昇が続いている（図表 4）。金融市場は財政拡張に対して神経を尖らせているが、秋季予算で
明確な財源が示さなければ、それを嫌気した投資家が英国債（ギルト債）の売却を進め、金利が一段と上昇し、
場合によってポンド安と株安の“トリプル安”を引き起こす可能性もある。

財政拡張の財源を増税で賄おうとすると、スターマー前政権のように有権者の支持を失いかねない。 一方、国
債発行で賄おうとすると、長期金利の押し上げ圧力になる。ゆえに、歳出の見直しで財源を賄おうとするだろうが、
簡単ではないため、具体的な筋道を示せなければ、結局は金利の上昇につながる。秋季予算で財政拡張の財
源の筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。

Document 960: 凱基投顧_6491 晶碩_莊政翰_20261020

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_6491 晶碩_莊政翰_20261020.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

Pages: 17
Status: ok

### Page 1

十月 20, 2026 研究網站： https：//investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  消費品 ‧ 台灣

晶碩 
(6491.TW /6491 TT)

多重成長動能推升獲利上升循環 
重要訊息  
我們認為晶碩將進入多年獲利上升循環，受惠 隱形眼鏡產業委外代工的結構
性趨勢以及高階鏡片產品滲透率提升。我們預期 隨著中國產能提升 及越南新
廠投產將帶動利潤率擴張， 並推升 2026-28F 獲利年複合成長率 加速至 14%。 
評論及分析  
受惠委外代工趨勢。 我們認為晶碩是全球隱形眼鏡產業委外代工結構性趨勢
的主要受惠者。 隨著品牌商愈來愈聚焦品牌經營與線上通路，製造與產品開
發則交由代工夥伴。這個趨勢在快速成長的彩片市場尤其明顯，因為產品週
期短加上組合複雜度高，更適合委外生產。晶碩日本營收強勁成長，反映公
司對既有品牌與新興品牌而言，作為製造夥伴的重要性持續提升。晶碩產能
持續擴充、產品組合完整，又已取得全球多地證照，我們認為可望進一步掌
握委外商機。  
受惠產品升級 週期。除了委外代工趨勢，我們認為鏡片高階化是晶碩另一項
重要的獲利成長動能。人口高齡化、螢幕使用時間增加、護眼意識提升，帶
動全球矽水膠、散光、多焦點等高階鏡片需求成長， 國際大廠這類產品的營
收成長高於整體鏡片即為佐證。晶碩高階產品合計營收佔比已超過  20%，
近期取得中國矽水膠證照後，明年可望提升至 25%，有利利潤率展望。  
多元生產佈局支撐利潤率擴張。 我們認為晶碩在台灣、中國及越南擴大生產
佈局，將強化長期成本優勢與利潤率結構。在中國當地生產可提升交貨效
率，並降低關稅與物流成本；越南新廠則可帶來額外的人力成本優勢與生產
彈性。隨著海外廠規模擴大，我們預期製造效率提升與成本結構改善，將支
撐 2027 年以後的利潤率顯著擴張。  
投資建議  
我們首評晶碩給予「增加持股」評等，根據 16 倍本益比設定目標價 525
元。在委外代工及產品升級趨勢下，我們認為晶碩的產能擴充 與多元生
產佈局將帶動公司進入多年獲利上升循環。  
投資風險 
矽水膠鏡片滲透率 低於預期；同業削價競爭。

### Page 2

台灣 晶碩

十月 20, 2026 研究網站： https：//investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

受惠產品升級趨勢 
我們首次評等晶碩，給予「增加持股」評等，目標價 525 元，係根據 16 倍
本益比  (與五年平均相當 )。我們認為晶碩未來幾年將進入獲利上升循環，預
期獲利年複合成長率將由 2023-25年的-0.8%，加速至 2026-28年的 14%。
晶碩在台灣、中國及越南持續增加產能，加上產品組合完整、取得全球多地
證照，我們認為可望掌握全球隱形眼鏡品牌擴大委外代工 的商機，帶動市佔
率提升並支撐長期成長。  
我們認為隨著消費者對矽水膠以及散光與多焦點等高階光學鏡片的採用率持
續提高  (相較傳統的水膠鏡片具有更高的價格與利潤率 )，正意味著產業產品
升級的結構性趨勢將支持利潤率持 續擴張趨勢。 
晶碩股價年初至今上漲 45%，同期台灣加權指數上漲 59%。不過目前本益
比 13.2 倍仍低於五年平均 16.2 倍，且接近歷史交易區間 12-30 倍的下緣。
考量公司獲利成長前景穩健、獲利能力持續提升，並受惠委外代工及產品高
階化等產業結構性利多，我們認為目前評價具吸引力，尚未完全反映長期成
長潛力。我們認為評價 具上修空間，理由包括： (1) 委外趨勢帶動市佔率提
升；(2) 利潤率持續擴張；(3) 獲利維持成長；(4) 利潤率較佳的高階產品營收
佔比提升。

### Page 4

台灣 晶碩

十月 20, 2026 研究網站： https：//investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

投資論點 
委外代工趨勢主要受惠者  
晶碩為台灣市值與產能最大的隱形眼鏡製造商，我們認為將受惠全球隱形眼
鏡產業持續的委外代工趨勢。隨著電商通路與線上 品牌快速成長，隱形眼鏡
品牌愈來愈聚焦於品牌經營，將生產外包給具備產品開發能力和 完整證照的
專業代工夥伴，已成為品牌日益重要的策略差異化關鍵。此趨勢在快速成長
的彩片市場尤其明顯，因為彩片產 品品項複雜度更高、產品更迭週期較快 ，
需要更具彈性的生產能力。  
台灣主要的隱形眼鏡 OEM/ODM 廠正在日本和中國等關鍵市場取得進展 。當
地新興的在地線上品牌正逐步從國際大廠手中搶下市佔，並日益 倚重委外生
產夥伴，以加快新品上市並 降低資本需求，因此這些品牌正將愈來愈多訂單
流向台灣代工廠。  
我們認為晶碩將是全球隱形眼鏡產業委外趨勢的主要受惠者。根據我們估
計，晶碩日本營收過去三 年年複合成長率達 20%，明顯優於整體市場及日本
當地同業，帶動日本營收佔比由 2Q23 的 49%提升至 2Q26 的 60-65%。晶
碩日本營收強勁成長，凸顯對既有品牌與新興線上 品牌而言，公司 作為製造
夥伴的重要性持續提升。  
展望未來，隨著隱形眼鏡品牌愈來愈倚賴代工夥伴以 提供生產彈性、加快新
品上市週期，我們預期晶碩代工營收將持續成長。憑藉著 產能持續擴充、完
整的產品組合，以及多元 的全球證照，我們認為晶碩可望在委外 代工商機中
取得更大份額。

受惠產品升級週期 
除了有利的委外 代工趨勢，我們認為 新一輪的產品升級循環為晶碩長期成長
的另一大支柱。鑒於人口高齡化、使用消費性電子產品的時間拉長，加上大
眾對眼睛健康意識的提升，國際隱形眼鏡廠商持續將產品轉向矽水膠、散光
及多焦等高階品項。CooperVision ( 美) 散光及多焦鏡片營收 2020- 25 年年複
合成長率達 11.3%，高於整體視力保健事業的 8.3%。高階產品成長動能延
續，2Q26 佔視力保健營收比重達 50.4%，高於三年前的 47.6%。同樣的在

日本市場， Seed ( 日) 2Q26 散光及多焦鏡片合計佔日拋鏡片營收比重為

散光/多焦鏡片營收，百萬美元 (左軸)；佔總營收比重，百分比 (右軸)   日拋鏡片產品別比重，百分比

鏡片升級至高階矽水膠產品的趨勢同樣明顯，反映消費者愈來愈重視配戴舒
適度、眼睛健康及視覺 表現。Bausch & Lomb ( 美) 的矽水膠鏡片營收過去兩
年複合成長率達 20.7%，顯著高於整體隱形眼鏡業務的 7.9%，其中矽水膠
日拋鏡片年複合成長率更高達 42%。Menicon (日 ) 矽水膠鏡片營收五年複合
成長率達 18.2%，且矽水膠佔日拋鏡片營收比重由 FY19 (財年止於三月) 的
6.1%，翻倍增長至 FY24 的 14.1%。此外，矽水膠彩片產品線的擴充預計將
擴大潛在市場規模，並加速 亞洲區域的產品升級循環。  
我們認為晶碩憑藉已取得全球多地矽水膠鏡片證照，將有助受惠持續的產品
升級趨勢。公司預期矽水膠產品的 營收比重將從目前的 7-8%提升至年底的
10%，隨著矽水膠產品近期在中國獲得證照 ，並預計 2027 年開始出貨，明
年目標比重將提升至 15%。 
由於矽水膠鏡片平均售價較高、利潤率 較佳，我們認為高階產品營收佔比提
升不僅有助營收成長，也將帶動產品組合優化，支撐未來數年毛利率擴張及
獲利成長。

十月 20, 2026 研究網站： https：//investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

多元生產佈局支撐利潤率進一步擴張  
晶碩生產基地原本集中在台灣，之後擴展至中國，目前正在越南興建新廠。
我們認為日益多元的生產佈局將為公司長期利潤率提供有力支撐。中國及越
南的人力與營運成本普遍低於台灣，晶碩擴大當地生產， 不僅可改善成本結
構，也同時提升營運彈性與供應鏈韌性。隨 著海外廠規模擴大、學習曲線推
進，我們預期生產 效率提升將支撐毛利率擴張。  
相較由台灣出口，在中國當地生產可縮短交期，並降低物流及關稅相關費
用。我們認為這將強化晶碩服務中國當地客戶的效率，並提升在中國市場的
競爭力。公司今年將 南通廠月產能擴充 300 萬片，總產能達 800 萬片。今年
取得矽水膠證照後，隨中國需求成長、客戶訂單加速，我們認為在地生產的
優勢將逐步顯現，支撐公司長期利潤率展望。  
展望未來，我們認為預計 2027 年底投產的越南新廠是未來利潤率擴張的關
鍵動能。越南廠人力成本更具競爭力，供應中國及其他出口市場時，也可降
低物流與關稅費用。更重要的是，越南廠預計可出貨至中國、日本及歐洲等
多個地區，提供更高的生產彈性與地域分散效益。根據我們估計， 2028 年
中國及越南合計產能將佔總產能約 14%，足以支應大部分中國需求。我們認
為日益在地化且多元的生產佈局，將強化晶碩的成本競爭力，支撐中長期利
潤率改善。

產業展望 
彩片熱潮帶動亞洲成長優於全球  
全球隱形眼鏡市場穩定成長，2025 年全球四大品牌銷售額達 105 億美元，
過去六年年複合成長率為 4.2%。除了近視率上升、數位裝置使用增加及人
口高齡化等結構性需求動能，產品創新與消費偏好轉變也支撐銷售成長。我
們認為亞洲需求將持續優於全球，動能來自美瞳及彩片日益普及、高階材質
採用率提升，以及特殊功能產品需求增加。彩片已從視力矯正工具演變為時
尚美妝配件，在中國及日本尤其明顯，當地新興 線上品牌加速了年輕族群的
採用。

線上通路擴大市場 滲透 
電商銷售比重提升讓產品更容易取得、觸及更多消費者，支撐亞洲隱形眼鏡
市場成長。中國線上平台已成為隱形眼鏡的主要銷售通路，天貓、京東、抖
音等平台約佔銷售額 80%，高於 2019 年的 52.5%。線上通路也讓品牌能推
出新設計、與消費者互動，並更快回應喜 好的變化。這個趨勢帶動更多元的
產品需求，也為具備彈性產線與高效率製程、能支援快速產品開發並縮短交
期的代工廠創造商機。

更專業的視力矯正需求。歐美矽水膠滲透率已超過 70%，亞洲僅 15-20%，

投資風險 
高階鏡片產品滲透不如預期。 矽水膠、散光及多焦點鏡片等高毛利產品的滲
透率提升，是公司長期成長策略的關鍵。若因價格考量、消費偏好改變或競
爭加劇，導致採用速度低於預期，產品組合與獲利能力的預期改善可能無法
實現。 
日本委外代工訂單依賴度高。 日本品牌持續加速委外代工 ，對公司長期成長
策略至關重要。若品牌自建產能或法規變動，導致委外趨勢放緩，可能降低
訂單能見度，並影響公司獲利成長展望。  
產能擴充不如預期。晶碩目前在台灣、中國及越南擴充產能。若設備安裝、
良率提升、人員訓練或客戶認證進度延遲，新產能的貢獻可能遞延。我們的
投資論點部分建立在營運槓桿與規模經濟上，產能擴張 若遇阻礙，營收成長
與利潤率擴張都可能承壓。  
匯率風險。日圓疲弱可能不利產業需求。台灣代工廠多以美元收款，日本品
牌及通路商營收則以日圓計價，日圓貶值等於推升客戶的採購成本， 這可能
壓縮客戶在行銷與產品推廣上的投入，或遞延新品上市，進而抑制消費需求
與產品滲透，拖累後續訂單成長。

### Page 10

台灣 晶碩

十月 20, 2026 研究網站： https：//investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 10

公司簡介 
產品線完整的隱形眼鏡製造商 
晶碩為台灣市值與產能最大的隱形眼鏡製造商，生產拋棄式軟式隱形眼鏡，
產品涵蓋水膠、矽水膠、彩片、散光及多焦點鏡片。公司 為全球隱形眼鏡品
牌的 OEM/ODM 供應商，自有品牌 則佔營收約 20%。憑藉先進的自動化生
產平台、強大的研發能力，以及在全球主要市場取得的相關 證照，晶碩已成
為亞洲領先的隱形眼鏡製造商之一。  
公司的生產基地 分布於台灣與中國。其中台灣主要 廠區龜山廠目前產能 滿載
運作，月產能達 9,000 萬片；大溪廠目前的月產能則為 1,500 萬片，預計
2026 年底提升至 2,000 萬片。中國江蘇省南通市設有一座工廠，月產能為
800 萬片，主要供應中國內需市場。展望未來，公司正 興建越南新廠，月產
能規劃 1,000 萬片，預計 2027 年底投產，屆時將有助支援中國市場以及其
他海外地區。 
日本為晶碩最大市場，佔營收比重 60-65%，其次為中國 10-15%、歐洲約
7.5%，其餘來自台灣。受惠彩片需求強勁及矽水膠鏡片滲透率提升，日本
市場一直是公司主要的營收成長動能，根據我們估計，過去三 年年複合成長
率達 20%。隨高階散光及多焦點鏡片需求增加，公司預期 2027 年日本營收
將維持雙位數成長。歐洲近期成為成長最快的地區，受惠高階光學鏡片及矽
水膠產品滲透率提升。中國則是長期的成長機會，動能來自隱形眼鏡使用人
口增加、彩片市場持續擴大，以及消費者愈來愈重視舒適度與眼睛健康，帶
動需求逐步轉向高價值的矽水膠鏡片。

晶碩的主要競爭優勢在於業界領先的自動化程度、強大的產品開發能力以及
在全球多地取得的相關 證照。高度自動化的產線能有效率地生產少量多樣的
客製化產品，同時維持高品質與成本競爭力。此外公司已取得日本、美國、
歐洲、中國及台灣等主要市場證照，形成相當高的進入門檻，並強化客戶黏
著度。加上產能持續擴充、高階鏡片比重提升，這些優勢將有助晶碩在全球
隱形眼鏡產業持續擴大市佔率。

台灣 晶碩

獲利展望 
產品升級循環帶動營收成長 
受惠客戶訂單強勁及產能持續擴充，3Q26 營收延續強勁成長，季增 6%、
年增 19%達 22.5 億元，創歷史新高。管理層表示儘管產能持續增加，現有
產能已接近滿載，客戶需求仍高於可供應的產能。公司預期台灣新增產能將
迅速被需求填滿，並展望 4Q26 營收將延續季增，優於 過往季節性。  
隨委外代工需求增加，以及日本與歐洲市場矽水膠、散光及多焦鏡片等高階
產品的滲透率提升，我們認為晶碩可望取得高於產業平均的成長並優化產品
組合，支撐未來數年營收成長與利潤率擴張。公司已在中國取得矽水膠透明
片證照，預計 2027 年開始出貨。由於中國矽水膠滲透率低於日本等成熟市
場，管理層將中國矽水膠視為 未來重要成長動能。此外中國價格競爭已趨穩
定，高階產品需求持續增加，有利高階鏡片滲透。因此公司預期 2027 年營
收將維持雙位數年增。  
矽水膠及高階產品 2Q26 已佔晶碩營收逾 20%，由於高階光學鏡片在多數市
場的採用仍處相對早期階段，未來成長空間可期。管理層指出，使用者改戴
散光及多焦產品後，視力通常明顯改善，有助提高產品接受度並推升採用率。
此外，矽水膠日拋鏡片透氧性較佳、配戴更舒適，加上護眼意識提升，在亞
洲愈來愈受青睞。我們認為高階產品營收佔比提升，將在未來數年同時帶動
營收成長及產品組合優化。

產品組合優化支撐利潤率擴張 
夏季電價較高加上 原物料成本上升，可能壓抑 3Q26 毛利率。不過 2Q26 認
列一次性庫藏股員工酬勞費用後 ，我們預期 3Q26 營業利益率將維持上季水
準。展望未來，我們看好晶碩的利潤率展望，主要受惠產品組合優化，尤其
是矽水膠、散光及多焦鏡片營收佔比提升。此外，產能利用率維持高檔、擴
產持續進行，可望提升營運槓桿。長期而言，中國及越南產能放量，可透過
較低的人力、物流及關稅相關費用強化成本競爭力，支撐毛利率持續擴張。
我們預估 2026 年毛利率將提升 0.7 個百分點，2027 -28年分別再提升 0.2 及
0.6 個百分點，帶動未來兩年獲利年複合成長率達 14%。

[台湾]～世界的な AI・半導体関連需要の旺盛さがけん引役となり、９月の輸出受注額は過去最高を更新～ 
20 日に発表された９月の輸出受注額は前年同月比＋62.4％となり、 前月 （同＋71.4％） から伸び
が鈍化した。 しかし、 前月比 （季調済） は＋1.1％と前月 （同＋5.9％） から伸びが鈍化したものの、
拡大基調を維持しており、単月ベースの輸出受注額は過去最高を更新している。品目別では、光学
機器関連の受注に下押し圧力が掛かっているものの、 半導体をはじめとする電子部品関連の受注が
引き続き旺盛な動きをみせているほか、情報通信関連、電気製品関連、化学製品関連などでも拡大
の動きが続いている。国・地域別でも、最大の受注先である米国向けが大きく拡大しているほか、
中国本土・ 香港向けや欧州向けも堅調に推移し、ASEAN（東南アジア諸国連合） 向けなど新興国向け

### Page 2

2/4 
も拡大しており、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛さが輸出受注を押し上げている
様子がうかがえる。 
23 日に発表された９月の失業率（季調済）は3.33％となり、前月（3.32％）から0.01pt 悪化し
た。失業者数は前月比＋0.1 万人と前月（同▲0.1 万人）から２ヵ月ぶりの増加に転じている。既
卒者、新卒者問わず全般的に増加基調に転じているほか、自発的失業、非自発的失業ともに増加す
る動きが確認されている。一方の雇用者数は前月比▲0.4 万人と前月（同＋0.2 万人）から２ヵ月
ぶりの減少に転じており、 世界的なＡＩ・半導体需要の旺盛さを反映して製造業で持ち直しの兆し
がみられるほか、建設業では堅調さがうかがえるものの、サービス業で頭打ち感が出ている。雇用
環境に変化の兆しが出ていることを受けて、 労働力人口は前月比▲0.2 万人と前月 （同＋0.1 万人）
から２ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じており、労働市場には一部で弱含みの動きがうかがえる。

[ニュージーランド]～主力の乳製品や食肉を中心に輸出は堅調も、輸入は頭打ち感が強まる～ 
19 日に発表された９月の輸出額は前年同月比＋12.3％となり、 前月 （同＋12.4％） からわずかに
伸びが鈍化した。 しかし、 前月比は＋0.6％と前月 （同＋5.2％） から増加ペースが鈍化したものの、
２ヵ月連続で増加するなど持ち直しの動きが続いている。 財別では、 原油価格の調整を反映して原
油の輸出額が大きく下振れしたほか、 機械製品関連や果物の輸出額も下振れしたものの、 主力の輸
出財である乳製品や食肉の輸出が堅調に推移していることが輸出全体を下支えしている。 国・地域
別では、米国向けやＥＵ向け、日本向けなどが軒並み低調となる一方、最大の輸出先である中国向
けやＡＳＥＡＮ諸国向けが堅調に推移しているほか、 隣国オーストラリア向けにも底堅さがうかが
える。一方、輸入額は前年同月比＋17.7％となり、前月（同＋13.2％）から伸びが加速した。ただ
し、前月比は▲0.2％と前月（同▲7.9％）に続いて２ヵ月連続で減少しており、頭打ち感が強まっ
ている。財別では、原油・石油製品関連の輸入が堅調に推移する一方、機械製品関連のほか、プラ
スチック関連などの素材・部材関連の輸入が下振れしたことが輸入全体の重しとなっている。 その
結果、貿易収支は▲19.71 億ＮＺドルと前月（▲15.36 億ＮＺドル）から赤字幅が拡大した。 
図 1 TW 輸出受注額の推移        図 2 TW 雇用環境の推移

（出所）CEIC より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成  （出所）CEIC より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

### Page 3

3/4

[マレーシア]～世界的な AI・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを追い風に、９月の輸出入額はともに過去最高に～ 
19 日に発表された９月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋1.8％となり、 前月 （同＋1.9％） からわずか
に伸びが鈍化した。前月比も＋0.07％と前月（同＋0.29％）から上昇ペースが鈍化しており、政府
による電気料金の負担軽減措置などを背景に住居・光熱費関連の物価上昇率が鈍化したほか、 食料
品価格の上昇の動きも一服しており、 生活必需品を中心にインフレ圧力が後退したことが影響して
いる。 なお、 価格変動の大きい生鮮食品や政府の価格管理対象品目などを除いたコアインフレ率は
前年同月比＋1.7％となり、 前月 （同＋1.7％） から２ヵ月連続で横ばいとなった。 前月比は＋0.29％
と前月（同＋0.29％）と同じペースで上昇しており、基調的な物価上昇圧力は根強く残っている。
一方、 輸送関連の物価上昇率が加速するなど、 エネルギー価格の上昇による影響が一部で顕在化し
ているほか、外食・宿泊関連の物価上昇率も加速しており、物価の動きには品目ごとにばらつきが
生じている。 
同日に発表された９月の輸出額は前年同月比＋54.5％となり、前月（同＋45.5％）から伸びが加
速した。 前月比 （季節調整値） も＋10.0％と前月 （同＋1.3％） から上昇ペースが加速して３ヵ月連
続で拡大しており、 単月ベースの輸出額は過去最高を更新するなど堅調な動きをみせている。 財別
では、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛さを反映して、 半導体など電子部品関連の
輸出が大幅に押し上げられたほか、 原油や天然ガスなどの価格上昇を受けて、 鉱物資源関連の輸出
額も拡大した。一方、パーム油をはじめとする農産品関連の輸出は減少したほか、化学製品関連の
輸出も弱含むなど、 財ごとの動きにはばらつきがみられる。 一方の輸入額は前年同月比＋52.7％と
なり、 前月 （同＋41.1％） から伸びが加速した。 前月比 （季節調整値） も＋11.9％と前月 （同＋1.2％）
から上昇ペースが加速して４ヵ月連続で拡大しており、 単月ベースの輸入額も過去最高を更新した。
輸出の堅調さを反映して、 生産活動に必要な機械製品関連の輸入が大幅に押し上げられたほか、 化
学製品関連などの素材・部材の需要も旺盛に推移している。さらに、原油価格などの上昇を受けて
鉱物資源関連の輸入額も押し上げられている。 その結果、 貿易収支は＋337.24 億リンギとなり、 前
月（＋280.85 億リンギ）から黒字幅が拡大した。 
図 3 NZ 貿易動向の推移

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4

[シンガポール]～生活必需品やサービス物価に上昇圧力は残るも、インフレ率・コアインフレ率は鈍化～ 
23 日に発表された９月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋2.2％となり、 前月 （同＋2.3％） からわずか
に伸びが鈍化した。前月比も＋0.21％と前月（同＋0.62％）から上昇ペースが鈍化したものの、生
鮮品をはじめとする食料品価格は上昇しているほか、電気・ガス価格も高水準で推移するなど、生
活必需品では物価上昇圧力が根強く残る動きがみられる。 住居費と民間輸送費を除いたコアインフ
レ率も前年同月比＋2.0％となり、前月（同＋2.2％）から伸びが鈍化した。前月比も＋0.06％と前
月（同＋0.27％）から上昇ペースが鈍化しており、エネルギー価格の高止まりを反映して輸送関連
サービスの価格に押し上げ圧力がかかる一方、 金融市場におけるＳＧドルの名目実効為替レートの
上昇が輸入物価の上昇圧力を和らげる方向に作用していると考えられる。 さらに、 家具や衣料品な
どの小売関連を中心に財価格の上昇圧力が和らいでいるほか、 サービス物価も上昇ペースが鈍化し
ている。一方で、食料品や一部のサービス価格には上昇圧力が根強く残っており、物価の動きにば
らつきが生じている。

Subject: ⼤跌合理嗎？搜尋⼜要被 AI 顛覆了？雲的增⻑應該怎樣理解？  財報解剖 : Google| 26 年 10 ⽉
